# Test classification — inventory only

Ticket: https://app.hypertask.ai/detail/project-4060/677

Pinned production tree: `5f70ce7b5284fe1697f202bd49d9a0ef63e633e2`; as of 2026-10-24T19:19:55Z. Reproduce with `node scripts/test-inventory/inventory.mjs --check`. See [method and limitations](../../scripts/test-inventory/README.md). Complete per-assertion lines, titles (including dynamic templates), guarded paths, history links (commit SHA), sampled overlap candidates and CI log line evidence are in [inventory.json](inventory.json); sanitized read-only GitHub inputs are in [evidence.json](evidence.json).

## Reconciled numbers

1555 test/spec files under tests/ (helpers and fixtures without a test/spec suffix are excluded, including reads made only by helpers). 858 files have a recognized source-read flow; 675 have directly source-tainted assertions. These are current-tree measurements, not the ticket's older approximate counts. 8 files need source-flow/no-direct-assertion review. Classification is a static heuristic, **not proof of behavioural quality**.

| Class | Files | Files with attributed timing | Reported callback seconds |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| behaviour | 874 | 821 | 729.487962 |
| source-text | 226 | 224 | 48.956561 |
| mixed | 455 | 453 | 739.335519 |

**Timing denominator:** one successful production full-suite job, [120954146642](https://github.com/hypertask-ai/hypertask/actions/runs/40120633811/job/120954146642), commit `fb68ca3d0d42f19523aa6172b04820a5c70fe5d9`. Full-suite step wall time: **1014 s**. Matched top-level callback/file-result durations are summed, not divided by four workers and not presented as removable wall time. Total top-level reported duration: **2632.938935 s** (20766 records), reconciled as the three classes plus unmatched-file and unattributed seconds. Unattributed top-level callback duration: **1115.158905 s** (10345 records). Timed historical files absent from this tree: **0 s**. Nested callbacks are excluded to avoid double counting; dynamic/duplicate names remain unattributed. Process startup, setup, teardown, tsx overhead and idle/concurrency overlap cannot be apportioned from this reporter. Missing file timing is **unknown, never zero cost**. No full suite was run locally.

**History denominator:** 1239 completed production push CI Tests runs in 2026-09-24T19:19:55Z through 2026-10-24T19:19:55Z; 765 ci-tests jobs inspected; 11 logs parsed; 0 logs unavailable. 8 current files have an attributed failure. A green job does not prove no failures before this window. Cancelled/in-progress runs and expired logs are not negative evidence. Git history is all history reachable from the pinned production tree, exact path only (no rename following); bugfix association uses a conservative 30-day commit-subject/path/ticket match. A failure proves a test can go red, **not that it caught a production defect**.

## Decision policy

KEEP 1555; REWRITE 0; DELETE 0. This is a conservative first inventory, **not a deletion/rewrite authorization**. Past tickets in filenames/test names (plus numeric filename suffixes), access/security tests with their allowed cases, and recent bugfix associations are forced KEEP. Every other existing test remains KEEP until a guarded-behaviour revert and equivalent behaviour coverage are established. This preserves every test that would fail under a revert without pretending to have run thousands of mutations. Git association, source inspection, shared target and a historical red are not causal regression proof. Overlap is recorded as candidates, not an unsubstantiated yes/no. The report explicitly retains unknowns instead of inventing "never failed", "duplicate", or "cannot fail".

## One row per test file

"No attributed failure" means unknown outside the queried/retained logs. "Revert not run" means can-fail-for-behaviour is unproven. A dash for timing is missing attribution. Titles are stated intent, not verified protection. Full details for every row are in inventory.json.
